## 概率核心密度(空間分析工具) · 參數

**id**　f51abffc8459a7b91a-25

**english**　Probabilistic Kernel Density

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html

**parameter**　area_unit_scale_factor

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 地區單位(備選)
- 指定輸出密度值將使用的區域單位。默認單位將根據輸出空間引用的線性單位確定。默認單位將依據輸出空間參考的線性單位確定。您可以將其修改為適當的單位以轉換密度輸出。線性密度數值將長度和面積的單位轉換。如果不指定輸出空間引用,則輸出空間引用將與輸入空間特征類相同。默認輸出資料密度單位由輸出空間引用的線性單位確定。如果輸出線性單位是米,產出區域密度單位將設置為平方公里。如果輸出線性單位是英尺,輸出區域密度單位將被設定為平方英里。如果輸出區域單位不是英尺或米,則產出區域密度單位將被設置為平方形。即,輸出密度單位將是輸出空間參考線性單位的平方。例如,如果產出線性單位為厘米,輸出區域密度單位將為平方圖單位,這將導致平方厘米;如果輸出線性單位為公里,輸出區域密度單位為平方地圖單位,這將導致平方公里。廣場地圖單位——將使用輸出空間參照線性單位的平方。將使用平方英里-美國英里。將使用平方公里-千米。將使用Acres-美國英畝。將使用Hectres-Hectares-美國英畝。將使用平方英尺-美國腳;將使用平方英寸-美國英寸;將使用平方公尺-米;將使用平方厘米-時表;將使用平方毫米-毫米。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Area Units (Optional)", "Specifies the area units that will be used for the output density values. A default unit is determined based on the linear unit of the output spatial reference. You can change this to the appropriate unit to convert the density output. Values for line density convert the units of both length and area. If no output spatial reference is specified, the output spatial reference will be the same as the input feature class. The default output density units are determined by the linear units of the output spatial reference. If the output linear units are meters, the output area density units will be set to Square kilometers. If the output linear units are feet, the output area density units will be set to Square miles. If the output units are anything other than feet or meters, the output area density units will be set to Square map units. That is, the output density units will be the square of the linear units of the output spatial reference. For example, if the output linear units are centimeters, the output area density units will be Square map units, which will result in square centimeters. If the output linear units are kilometers, the output area density units will be Square map units, which will result in square kilometers. Square map units—The square of the linear units of the output spatial reference will be used. Square miles—U.S. miles will be used. Square kilometers—Kilometers will be used. Acres—U.S. acres will be used. Hectares—Hectares will be used. Square yards—U.S. yards will be used. Square feet—U.S. feet will be used. Square inches—U.S. inches will be used. Square meters—Meters will be used. Square centimeters—Centimeters will be used. Square millimeters—Millimeters will be used.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#parameters

**parameter**　output_cell_values

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 輸出單元格值( 可選)
- 指定輸出光柵代表的值。由于單元格值與單元格大小相關,因此不能將輸出光柵重新標為不同的單元格大小。密度 -輸出值代表每個單元格單位區域的計算密度值。這是默認值。預期計數 - 輸出值代表每個單元格區域的計算密度值。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Output Cell Values (Optional)", "Specifies what the values in the output raster represent. Because the cell value is linked to the cell size, the output raster cannot be resampled to a different cell size. Densities—The output values represent the calculated density value per unit area for each cell. This is the default. Expected counts—The output values represent the calculated density value per cell area.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#parameters

**english**　Probabilistic Kernel Density

- 工具參考

**parameter**　out_exceedance_probability_raster

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 概率快速(可選)
- 當單個單元格密度值超過臨界值時,輸出光柵與超常概率的超常概率將計算出超常概率。超高概率將作為超過閾值的模擬的分數來計算。值介于0至1之間。當指定該參數時,將啟用概率閾值百分位參數。
- 光柵資料集

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Output Exceedance Probability Raster (Optional)", "The output raster with the exceedance probability where individual cell density value exceeds the threshold value. The exceedance probability will be computed as the fraction of simulations that exceed the threshold. Values range from 0 to 1. When this parameter is specified, the Probability Threshold Percentile parameter will be enabled.", "Raster Dataset"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#parameters

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 概率閾值百分率(備選)
- 輸出密度光柵參數值的百分位數將用作輸出超常概率光柵參數的閾值。從完整密度表面計算一次閾值,因此不需要絕對密度值。默認值為90。只有當指定了輸出超常概率光柵參數值的有效路徑時,才可用此參數。
- 雙雙

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Probability Threshold Percentile (Optional)", "The percentile of the Output Density Raster parameter value that will be used as the threshold for the Output Exceedance Probability Raster parameter. The threshold is computed once from the completed density surface, so no absolute density value is required. The default is 90. This parameter is only available when a valid path is specified for the Output Exceedance Probability Raster parameter value.", "Double"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#parameters

**parameter**　uncertainty_type

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 不確定性類型 [不確定類型_類型,...]
- 指定在密度估計中傳播的不確定性源。可以選擇任何數值組合。每個選定的類型只支持自己的千米。SAMPLING-Sampalling-Samparing不確定性將表現為用替換來重新復制事件。當指定此選項時,將支持樣本_pct 參數。定位 -Pospical不確定性代表于以隨機數量抵消每個事件。當指定該選項時,將支持jitter_factor 參數。 ATTRIBUTE - 人口欄位重量中的不確定性代表于它們。 當指定該選項時, attr_error_type, 分布_method, 和 at將支持參數。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["uncertainty_type [uncertainty_type,...]", "Specifies the sources of uncertainty that will be propagated into the density estimate. Any combination of values can be selected. Each selected type supports only its own subparameters. SAMPLING—Sampling uncertainty will be represented by resampling the events with replacement. When this option is specified, the sample_pct parameter will be supported. LOCATION—Positional uncertainty is represented by offsetting each event by a random amount. When this option is specified, the jitter_factor parameter will be supported. ATTRIBUTE—Uncertainty in the population-field weights is represented by perturbing them. When this option is specified, the attr_error_type, distribution_method, and attr_error_pct parameters will be supported.", "String"], "section": "Return value"}

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

- 密度_計算_方法(可選)
- 指定用于將模擬表面歸納為主輸出光柵的統計。 表示 - 每個單元格模擬表面的平均值將被使用。 這是默認值 。 MDEIAN - 模擬表面的中位值將被使用 。 這個方法對于處理偶發極端模擬更為健全 。 MINIMUM - 每個單元格的最小模擬值將被使用。 此方法將產生比其他任何選項更保守的表面 。 MAXIMUM - 每個單元格的最大模擬值將被使用 。

MEAN—The mean of the simulated surfaces at each cell will be used. This is the default. MEDIAN—The median of the simulated surfaces will be used. This method is more robust for handling occasional extreme simulations. MINIMUM—The minimum simulated value at each cell will be used. This method will produce a more conservative surface compared to any other option. MAXIMUM—The maximum simulated value at each cell will be used.", "String"], "section": "Return value"}

## 概率核心密度(空間分析工具) · 返回返回值

**id**　f51abffc8459a7b91a-40

**english**　Probabilistic Kernel Density

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html

**parameter**　sampling_strategy

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 采樣_戰略(可選)
- 指定如何生成驅動模擬的隨機抽取。 所有策略都是不偏向的, 在估算穩定速度方面各不相同。 RANDOM - 每個隨機抽取都具有相同的選擇概率。 此策略確保所有可能的結果都有同樣的可能性。 這是默認的。 亞伯林- 隨機抽取是通過子抽樣現有資料產生的。 這個策略可以減少計算負載, 同時仍然提供公正的估計。 FARTHEST_POINT_SAMPLing - 隨機抽取是使用最遠點取樣戰略生成的, 其中選擇了與先前選定點最遙遠的點。 這一策略可以比純隨機采樣更快地穩定估計數。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["sampling_strategy (Optional)", "Specifies how the random draws that drive the simulations are generated. All strategies are unbiased. They differ in how quickly the estimates stabilize. RANDOM—Each random draw has an equal probability of being selected. This strategy ensures that all possible outcomes are equally likely. This is the default. SUBSAMPLING—The random draws are generated by subsampling the available data. This strategy can reduce computational load while still providing unbiased estimates. FARTHEST_POINT_SAMPLING—The random draws are generated using the farthest point sampling strategy, which selects points that are maximally distant from previously selected points. This strategy can lead to faster stabilization of estimates compared to purely random sampling.", "String"], "section": "Return value"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

- 資料類型

**values**　
- 抽樣_方法(可選)
- 指定如何選擇和替換抽樣點,并從輸入資料中替換這些點。該參數僅在不確定性類型參數設定為 SAMMPLING 選項時才可用。STANDARD_BOOOTSTRAP - 用替換來復制事件以代表取樣的不確定性。這是默認的。SMOOTH_BOOTSTRAP - 添加替換小內核噪聲并增加小內核噪音,使重標點的位置可以移位。

This parameter is only available when the uncertainty_type parameter is set to the SAMPLING option. STANDARD_BOOTSTRAP—Resamples the events with replacement to represent sampling uncertainty. This is the default. SMOOTH_BOOTSTRAP—Resamples with replacement and adds small kernel noise that enables locational displacement for the resampled points.", "String"], "section": "Return value"}

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

**values**　
- jitter_ 圖徵( 可選)
- 位置偏移將作為搜索半徑的分數適用于每個事件。 設置它以反映輸入資料的位置準確性。 默認值為 0.05, 值介于0.001 至 1. 0。 此參數只有在不確定性_ 類型參數被設定到 LocATION 選項時才能使用 。

This parameter is only available when the uncertainty_type parameter is set to the LOCATION option.", "Double"], "section": "Return value"}

## 概率核心密度(空間分析工具) · 返回返回值

**id**　f51abffc8459a7b91a-44

**english**　Probabilistic Kernel Density

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html

**parameter**　attr_error_type

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- attr_error_ 類型 (可選擇)
- 指定如何定義人口-地區加權的不確定性。該參數只有在將不確定_類型參數設置到 ATTRIBUTE 選項時才能使用。 FIXED_PERCENT - 不確定性將被定義為占人口- 地區加權的固定百分比。允許根據一個不變百分比值對人口- 地區加權進行擾動。當指定此選項時,將支持 Attr_error_ type、 分布_ method 和 Attr_error_pct 參數。這是默認的。 MOE_FIELD - 不確定性將從一個空域邊際欄位中讀取并轉換為標準錯誤, 使用信任度。當指定此選項時,將支持moe_field 和moe_信心參數。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["attr_error_type (Optional)", "Specifies how the uncertainty in the population-field weights will be defined. This parameter is only available when the uncertainty_type parameter is set to the ATTRIBUTE option. FIXED_PERCENT—The uncertainty will be defined as a fixed percentage of the population-field weights. This allows for perturbation of the population-field weights based on a constant percentage value. When this option is specified, the attr_error_type, distribution_method, and attr_error_pct parameters will be supported. This is the default. MOE_FIELD—The uncertainty will be read from a margin-of-error field and converted to a standard error using the confidence level. When this option is specified, the moe_field, and moe_confidence parameters will be supported.", "String"], "section": "Return value"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

## 概率核心密度(空間分析工具) · 返回返回值

**id**　f51abffc8459a7b91a-45

**english**　Probabilistic Kernel Density

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html

**parameter**　distribution_method

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 分布方法( 可選)
- 指定用于擾動加權數的分布。所有選項都使用原始重量作為平均值,標準錯誤作為標準差錯作為擴散。修改分布只會改變不確定性的形狀。它不會改變重量的大小。當 ATTRIBUTE 被選為不確定性類型值時,該參數是可用的。 NORMAL - A 對稱法正態分布。 這是一個未限制的分布, 可能產生負值。 任何負值都將夾在零上。 這是默認值。 LOGNORMAL- 以正對數系數乘增加權數后產生的右偏斜分布。 它永遠不會產生負加權數。 UIFORM - A對稱分布, 間隔中每個值都有負值。TRIANGULAR - 約束性分布達到原重量峰值,并逐漸達到對稱極限。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["distribution_method (Optional)", "Specifies the distribution used to perturb the weights. All options use the original weight as the mean and the standard error as the spread. Changing the distribution will only change the shape of the uncertainty. It will not change the magnitude of the weight. This parameter is available when ATTRIBUTE is selected as the uncertainty_type value. NORMAL—A symmetric normal distribution. This is an unbounded distribution and may produce negative values. Any negative values will be clamped to zero. This is the default. LOGNORMAL—A right-skewed distribution generated by multiplying the weight by a positive lognormal factor. It will never produce negative weights. UNIFORM—A symmetrical distribution with every value in the interval having equal probabilities to be selected. TRIANGULAR—A bounded distribution peaked at the original weight and tapering to symmetric limits.", "String"], "section": "Return value"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

- 外地

**id**　f51abffc8459a7b91a-51

**english**　Probabilistic Kernel Density

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html

**parameter**　area_unit_scale_factor

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 區域_ 單位_ 尺度_ 因素(可選)
- 指定輸出密度值將使用的區域單位。根據輸出空間引用的線性單位確定默認單位。您可以將其修改為適當的單位,以轉換密度輸出。線性密度數值可以轉換長度和面積的單位。如果不指定輸出空間參考,輸出空間引用將與輸入空間特征類相同。默認輸出密度單位由輸出空間引用的直線單位決定。如果輸出線性單位是米,則產出區域密度單位將被設置為 SQUARE_KILOMEERS。如果輸出線性單位是英尺,則輸出區域密度單位將被設置為 SQUARE_MILES。如果輸出單位不是腳或其它東西,則產出區域密度單位將被設置為SQUARE_MILES。輸出區域密度單位將設置為 SQUARE_MAP_UNITS 。 也就是說, 產出密度單位將是輸出空間參考線性單位的平方。 例如, 如果輸出線性單位是厘米, 輸出區域密度單位將是 SQUARE_MAP_UNITS , 這將導致平方厘米。 如果輸出線性單位是公里, 輸出區域密度單位將是 SQUARE_MAP_UNITS , 這將導致平方公里。 SQUARE_ MAP_UNITS - 輸出空間引用線性單位的平方塊將被使用。 SQUARE_MILES- U. 英里。 SQUARE_ 。將使用千兆赫-美國英畝。 HECTARES-Heters-CENTIMETERS-Centimeters將使用。 SQUARE_YARDS-美國碼將使用。SQUARE_FEET-美國英尺將使用。SQUARE_INCHES-美國英寸將使用SQUARE_INCHES-美國英寸。SQUARE_METERS-Meters將使用。SQUARE_CENTIMETEERS-Centimeters將使用SQUARE_MILMETERs-Millemets將使用。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["area_unit_scale_factor (Optional)", "Specifies the area units that will be used for the output density values. A default unit is determined based on the linear unit of the output spatial reference. You can change this to the appropriate unit to convert the density output. Values for line density convert the units of both length and area. If no output spatial reference is specified, the output spatial reference will be the same as the input feature class. The default output density units are determined by the linear units of the output spatial reference. If the output linear units are meters, the output area density units will be set to SQUARE_KILOMETERS. If the output linear units are feet, the output area density units will be set to SQUARE_MILES. If the output units are anything other than feet or meters, the output area density units will be set to SQUARE_MAP_UNITS. That is, the output density units will be the square of the linear units of the output spatial reference. For example, if the output linear units are centimeters, the output area density units will be SQUARE_MAP_UNITS, which will result in square centimeters. If the output linear units are kilometers, the output area density units will be SQUARE_MAP_UNITS, which will result in square kilometers. SQUARE_MAP_UNITS—The square of the linear units of the output spatial reference will be used. SQUARE_MILES—U.S. miles will be used. SQUARE_KILOMETERS—Kilometers will be used. ACRES—U.S. acres will be used. HECTARES—Hectares will be used. SQUARE_YARDS—U.S. yards will be used. SQUARE_FEET—U.S. feet will be used. SQUARE_INCHES—U.S. inches will be used. SQUARE_METERS—Meters will be used. SQUARE_CENTIMETERS—Centimeters will be used. SQUARE_MILLIMETERS—Millimeters will be used.", "String"], "section": "Return value"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#return-value

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_features", "label_zh": "輸入點特征", "label_en": "Input Point Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input point features for which density will be calculated."}, {"parameter": "population_field", "label_zh": "人口和外地", "label_en": "Population Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field denoting population values for each point. The field can be a count or a quantity that will be used to spread across the surface to create a continuous density. If no field is provided, each point will be counted once."}, {"parameter": "uncertainty_type", "label_zh": "不確定性類型", "label_en": "Uncertainty Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the sources of uncertainty that will be propagated into the density estimate. Any combination of values can be selected. Each selected type reveals only its own subparameters on the dialog box. Sampling—Sampling uncertainty will be represented by resampling the events with replacement. Selecting this option enables the Percentage of Data to Sample parameter. Location—Positional uncertainty is represented by offsetting each event by a random amount. Selecting this option enables the Location Displacement Factor parameter. Attribute—Uncertainty in the population-field weights is represented by perturbing them. Selecting this option enables the Attribute Error Type, Distribution Method, and Attribute Error Percentage parameters."}, {"parameter": "density_calculation_method", "label_zh": "產出密度計算方法(備選)", "label_en": "Output Density Calculation Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the statistic that will be used to summarize the simulated surfaces into the primary output raster. Mean—The mean of the simulated surfaces at each cell will be used. This is the default. Median—The median of the simulated surfaces will be used. This method is more robust for handling occasional extreme simulations. Minimum—The minimum simulated value at each cell will be used. This method will produce a more conservative surface compared to any other option. Maximum—The maximum simulated value at each cell will be used."}, {"parameter": "n_sims", "label_zh": "模擬數(可選)", "label_en": "Number of Simulations (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of times the tool will generate output density surfaces. The aggregate surface will be reported using the selected Output Density Calculation Method option. The value can range from 10 to 10,0000. A larger number of simulations will produce a more stable mean and uncertainty estimates. The default number of simulations is 200."}, {"parameter": "seed_value", "label_zh": "隨機種子( 可選)", "label_en": "Random Seed (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The seed for the random draws. The same inputs and seed value will always produce identical results. Change the seed value for an independent but statistically consistent run. The default is 10."}, {"parameter": "sampling_strategy", "label_zh": "抽樣戰略(備選)", "label_en": "Sampling Strategy (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how the random draws that drive the simulations are generated. All strategies are unbiased. They differ in how quickly the estimates stabilize. Random—Each random draw has an equal probability of being selected. This strategy ensures that all possible outcomes are equally likely. This is the default. Subsampling—The random draws are generated by subsampling the available data. This strategy can reduce computational load while still providing unbiased estimates. Farthest point sampling—The random draws are generated using the farthest point sampling strategy, which selects points that are maximally distant from previously selected points. This strategy can lead to faster stabilization of estimates compared to purely random sampling."}, {"parameter": "sampling_method", "label_zh": "抽樣方法(備選)", "label_en": "Sampling Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how the sampled points are selected and replaced from the input data. This parameter is only available when the Uncertainty Type parameter is set to the Sampling option. Standard bootstrap—Resamples the events with replacement to represent sampling uncertainty. This is the default. Smooth bootstrap—Resamples with replacement and adds small kernel noise that enables locational displacement for the resampled points."}, {"parameter": "sample_pct", "label_zh": "資料占抽樣(備選)資料的百分比", "label_en": "Percentage of Data to Sample (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The size of the resampling as a percentage of the number of events. Lower values increase the variability between simulations and widen the uncertainty bands. This parameter is only available when the Uncertainty Type parameter is set to the Sampling option. The default is 90."}, {"parameter": "jitter_factor", "label_zh": "流離失所因素(可選)", "label_en": "Location Displacement Factor (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The positional offset that will be applied to each event as a fraction of the search radius. Set it to reflect the positional accuracy of the input data. The default is 0.05, and the value can range from 0.001 to 1.0. This parameter is only available when the Uncertainty Type parameter is set to the Location option."}, {"parameter": "attr_error_type", "label_zh": "屬性錯誤類型( 可選)", "label_en": "Attribute Error Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how the uncertainty in the population-field weights will be defined. This parameter is only available when the Uncertainty Type parameter is set to the Attribute option. Fixed percentage of value—The uncertainty will be defined as a fixed percentage of the population-field weights. This allows for perturbation of the population-field weights based on a constant percentage value. Selecting this option enables the Attribute Error Percentage parameter. This is the default. Margin-of-error field—The uncertainty will be read from a margin-of-error field and converted to a standard error using the confidence level. Selecting this option enables the Margin-of-Error Field and Margin-of-Error Confidence Level parameters."}, {"parameter": "distribution_method", "label_zh": "分發方法(可選)", "label_en": "Distribution Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the distribution used to perturb the weights. All options use the original weight as the mean and the standard error as the spread. Changing the distribution will only change the shape of the uncertainty. It will not change the magnitude of the weight. This parameter is available when Attribute is selected as the Uncertainty Type value. Normal—A symmetric normal distribution. This is an unbounded distribution and may produce negative values. Any negative values will be clamped to zero. This is the default. Lognormal—A right-skewed distribution generated by multiplying the weight by a positive lognormal factor. It will never produce negative weights. Uniform—A symmetrical distribution with every value in the interval having equal probabilities to be selected. Triangular—A bounded distribution peaked at the original weight and tapering to symmetric limits."}, {"parameter": "moe_field", "label_zh": "誤差欄位(可選)", "label_en": "Margin-of-Error Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "A numeric field giving the margin of error of each weight. This parameter is available when Margin-of-Error Field is selected as the Attribute Error Type parameter value."}, {"parameter": "moe_confidence", "label_zh": "誤差信任度(可選)", "label_en": "Margin-of-Error Confidence Level (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the confidence level of the margin of error (MOE) that will be used to convert it to a standard error. This parameter will be enabled when the Attribute Error Type parameter is set to Margin-of-error field. 90%—The margin of error corresponds to a 90% confidence level (z = 1.645). This is the default. 95%—The margin of error corresponds to a 95% confidence level (z = 1.96). 99%—The margin of error corresponds to a 99% confidence level (z = 2.576)."}, {"parameter": "attr_error_pct", "label_zh": "屬性錯誤 百分比(可選擇)", "label_en": "Attribute Error Percentage (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The percentage error applied to each weight to derive its standard error. This parameter will be enabled when the Attribute Error Type parameter value is Fixed Percentage of Value. The default is 10."}, {"parameter": "search_radius", "label_zh": "搜索半徑( 可選)", "label_en": "Search Radius (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The search radius (bandwidth) within which density will be calculated, in the linear units of the output spatial reference. Larger values produce a smoother, more generalized raster; smaller values show more detail. If left empty, a default value is computed from the spatial configuration and number of input points using a spatial variant of Silverman's Rule of Thumb."}, {"parameter": "cell_size", "label_zh": "輸出單元格大小( 可選)", "label_en": "Output Cell Size (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Analysis Cell Size", "description_en": "The cell size of the output rasters. It can be defined by a numeric value or obtained from an existing raster dataset. If not specified, it is derived from the environment or calculated as the shorter of the extent width or height divided by 250."}, {"parameter": "area_unit_scale_factor", "label_zh": "地區單位(備選)", "label_en": "Area Units (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the area units that will be used for the output density values. A default unit is determined based on the linear unit of the output spatial reference. You can change this to the appropriate unit to convert the density output. Values for line density convert the units of both length and area. If no output spatial reference is specified, the output spatial reference will be the same as the input feature class. The default output density units are determined by the linear units of the output spatial reference. If the output linear units are meters, the output area density units will be set to Square kilometers. If the output linear units are feet, the output area density units will be set to Square miles. If the output units are anything other than feet or meters, the output area density units will be set to Square map units. That is, the output density units will be the square of the linear units of the output spatial reference. For example, if the output linear units are centimeters, the output area density units will be Square map units, which will result in square centimeters. If the output linear units are kilometers, the output area density units will be Square map units, which will result in square kilometers. Square map units—The square of the linear units of the output spatial reference will be used. Square miles—U.S. miles will be used. Square kilometers—Kilometers will be used. Acres—U.S. acres will be used. Hectares—Hectares will be used. Square yards—U.S. yards will be used. Square feet—U.S. feet will be used. Square inches—U.S. inches will be used. Square meters—Meters will be used. Square centimeters—Centimeters will be used. Square millimeters—Millimeters will be used."}, {"parameter": "output_cell_values", "label_zh": "輸出單元格值( 可選)", "label_en": "Output Cell Values (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies what the values in the output raster represent. Because the cell value is linked to the cell size, the output raster cannot be resampled to a different cell size. Densities—The output values represent the calculated density value per unit area for each cell. This is the default. Expected counts—The output values represent the calculated density value per cell area."}, {"parameter": "method", "label_zh": "(備選)", "label_en": "Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the flat earth (planar) or the shortest path on a spheroid (geodesic) method will be used. Planar—The planar distance between features will be used. This is the default. Geodesic—The geodesic distance between features will be used."}, {"parameter": "out_standard_dev_raster", "label_zh": "輸出標準偏離加速(可選)", "label_en": "Output Standard Deviation Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The output raster with the standard deviation of density across simulations at each cell. It is a direct measure of uncertainty in the density units."}, {"parameter": "out_coefficient_variation_raster", "label_zh": "變異品種(可選)的輸出系數", "label_en": "Output Coefficient of Variation Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The output raster with the coefficient of variation (standard deviation divided by mean) at each cell. It expresses uncertainty relative to the estimate. Cells with a mean of zero or below are assigned NoData."}, {"parameter": "out_exceedance_probability_raster", "label_zh": "概率快速(可選)", "label_en": "Output Exceedance Probability Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The output raster with the exceedance probability where individual cell density value exceeds the threshold value. The exceedance probability will be computed as the fraction of simulations that exceed the threshold. Values range from 0 to 1. When this parameter is specified, the Probability Threshold Percentile parameter will be enabled."}, {"parameter": "prob_threshold_percentile", "label_zh": "概率閾值百分率(備選)", "label_en": "Probability Threshold Percentile (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The percentile of the Output Density Raster parameter value that will be used as the threshold for the Output Exceedance Probability Raster parameter. The threshold is computed once from the completed density surface, so no absolute density value is required. The default is 90. This parameter is only available when a valid path is specified for the Output Exceedance Probability Raster parameter value."}]

**signatures**　
- ProbabilisticKernelDensity(in_features, population_field, uncertainty_type, {density_calculation_method}, {n_sims}, {seed_value}, {sampling_strategy}, {sampling_method}, {sample_pct}, {jitter_factor}, {attr_error_type}, {distribution_method}, {moe_field}, {moe_confidence}, {attr_error_pct}, {search_radius}, {cell_size}, {area_unit_scale_factor}, {output_cell_values}, {method}, {out_standard_dev_raster}, {out_coefficient_variation_raster}, {out_exceedance_probability_raster}, {prob_threshold_percentile})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_features、population_field、uncertainty_type、density_calculation_method、n_sims、seed_value、sampling_strategy、sampling_method、sample_pct、jitter_factor、attr_error_type、distribution_method、moe_field、moe_confidence、attr_error_pct、search_radius、cell_size、area_unit_scale_factor、output_cell_values、method、out_standard_dev_raster、out_coefficient_variation_raster、out_exceedance_probability_raster、prob_threshold_percentile。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 概率核心密度(空間分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具

**english**　Probabilistic Kernel Density

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html

**id**　f51abffc8459a7b91a-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/probabilistic-kernel-density.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Probabilistic Kernel Density，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入點特征 [in_features]、人口和外地 [population_field]、不確定性類型 [uncertainty_type]、產出密度計算方法(備選) [density_calculation_method]、模擬數(可選) [n_sims]、隨機種子( 可選) [seed_value]、抽樣戰略(備選) [sampling_strategy]、抽樣方法(備選) [sampling_method]、資料占抽樣(備選)資料的百分比 [sample_pct]、流離失所因素(可選) [jitter_factor]、屬性錯誤類型( 可選) [attr_error_type]、分發方法(可選) [distribution_method]、誤差欄位(可選) [moe_field]、誤差信任度(可選) [moe_confidence]、屬性錯誤 百分比(可選擇) [attr_error_pct]、搜索半徑( 可選) [search_radius]、輸出單元格大小( 可選) [cell_size]、地區單位(備選) [area_unit_scale_factor]、輸出單元格值( 可選) [output_cell_values]、(備選) [method]、輸出標準偏離加速(可選) [out_standard_dev_raster]、變異品種(可選)的輸出系數 [out_coefficient_variation_raster]、概率快速(可選) [out_exceedance_probability_raster]、概率閾值百分率(備選) [prob_threshold_percentile]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.8

## 生成更新相關屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　f52059499d8f1843ae-2

**english**　Generate Update Related Attribute Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集
- 模板

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html

**text**　工具運行后，請導入屬性規則并將其應用到相應資料集中。 如果資料集位于企業級地理資料庫中，您必須是資料所有者才能應用輸出的模板屬性規則。 了解關于屬性規則導入的詳細信息
如果輸入表參數值或搜索選項參數的目標圖層值不是公共設施網絡類或表，則搜索類型值不能是公共設施網絡關聯類型。
如果輸入表參數值或搜索選項參數的目標圖層值是一個表，則搜索類型值不能為僅空間。
如果輸入表參數值和搜索選項參數的目標圖層值彼此不參與關系類，則搜索類型值不能為關系類。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html#usage

## 生成更新相關屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 參數

**id**　f52059499d8f1843ae-6

**english**　Generate Update Related Attribute Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集
- 模板

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html

**parameter**　search_options

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 搜索選項
- 使用公共設施網絡關聯、關系類、匹配欄位或輸入類與任何目標類之間的空間關系來更新目標圖層中的相關值。 值表列： Target Layer—The layer to which the search option will be applied. Target Field—The field in the target layer that will be updated by the attribute rule. Search Type—The search type that will be applied to the input table and target table. Relationship Class—A related value in the target class will be updated using a relationship class between the input class and any target classes. Match Fields—A field in the target classes will be updated based on updates to a field in the input class based on a match field (primary or foreign key relationship). Spatial Only—A field in a target layer will be updated based on the intersection between features from the input class and any output classes. Connected (UN Association)—The features connected to the specified feature through a connectivity association will be returned. Container (UN Association)—The features containing the specified feature through a containment association will be returned. Content (UN Association)—The features containing the specified feature through a containment association will be returned. Structure (UN Association)—The feature that the specified feature is attached to through a structural attachment association will be returned. Attached (UN Association)—The features attached to the specified feature through a structural attachment association will be returned. Junction Edge (UN Association)—The features connected to the specified feature through a junction-edge connectivity association will be returned. Midspan (UN Association)—The features connected to the specified feature through a junction-edge midspan connectivity association will be returned. Input Field or Value—The field or value that will be edited in the input class. Operator—The method that will be used to relate the target and input fields. Set Value—The value in the target field will be updated with the value from the input field. Concatenate—The existing value in the target field will be updated by concatenating with the value in the source field. By default, the values will concatenated with a hyphen (-) character. Bitwise Or—A bitwise operation will be performed on the existing value in the target field with the incoming value from the source field. Both the source and target fields must be numeric. Search Distance—The maximum distance from an input feature that a target feature can be. Only features within this distance will be updated. Target Match Fields—The foreign key field from the target class that links to the Input Match Field value in the input class. Input Match Fields—The primary key field from the source class that links to the Target Match Field value in the target class.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html#parameters

## 生成更新相關屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集
- 模板

**english**　Generate Update Related Attribute Rule

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html

**id**　f52059499d8f1843ae-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate Update Related Attribute Rule，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入表 [in_table]、輸出文件夾 [out_folder]、搜索選項 [search_options]、表達式 [where_clause]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**english**　Generate Update Related Attribute Rule

**id**　f52059499d8f1843ae-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/generate-update-related-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_table", "label_zh": "輸入表", "label_en": "Input Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table View", "description_en": "The table or feature class that will have the new rule applied."}, {"parameter": "out_folder", "label_zh": "輸出文件夾", "label_en": "Output Folder", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Folder", "description_en": "The folder where the output will be saved."}, {"parameter": "search_options", "label_zh": "搜索選項", "label_en": "Search Options", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Updates a related value in the target layer using either a utility network association, relationship class, match fields, or spatial relationship between the input class and any target classes. Value table columns: Target Layer—The layer to which the search option will be applied. Target Field—The field in the target layer that will be updated by the attribute rule. Search Type—The search type that will be applied to the input table and target table. Relationship Class—A related value in the target class will be updated using a relationship class between the input class and any target classes. Match Fields—A field in the target classes will be updated based on updates to a field in the input class based on a match field (primary or foreign key relationship). Spatial Only—A field in a target layer will be updated based on the intersection between features from the input class and any output classes. Connected (UN Association)—The features connected to the specified feature through a connectivity association will be returned. Container (UN Association)—The features containing the specified feature through a containment association will be returned. Content (UN Association)—The features containing the specified feature through a containment association will be returned. Structure (UN Association)—The feature that the specified feature is attached to through a structural attachment association will be returned. Attached (UN Association)—The features attached to the specified feature through a structural attachment association will be returned. Junction Edge (UN Association)—The features connected to the specified feature through a junction-edge connectivity association will be returned. Midspan (UN Association)—The features connected to the specified feature through a junction-edge midspan connectivity association will be returned. Input Field or Value—The field or value that will be edited in the input class. Operator—The method that will be used to relate the target and input fields. Set Value—The value in the target field will be updated with the value from the input field. Concatenate—The existing value in the target field will be updated by concatenating with the value in the source field. By default, the values will concatenated with a hyphen (-) character. Bitwise Or—A bitwise operation will be performed on the existing value in the target field with the incoming value from the source field. Both the source and target fields must be numeric. Search Distance—The maximum distance from an input feature that a target feature can be. Only features within this distance will be updated. Target Match Fields—The foreign key field from the target class that links to the Input Match Field value in the input class. Input Match Fields—The primary key field from the source class that links to the Target Match Field value in the target class."}, {"parameter": "where_clause", "label_zh": "表達式", "label_en": "Expression (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "SQL Expression", "description_en": "An SQL expression that will be used to limit the features from the input that will participate in the attribute rule."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.GenerateUpdateRelatedAttributeRule(in_table, out_folder, search_options, {where_clause})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_table、out_folder、search_options、where_clause。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 查找點聚類 (GeoAnalytics Server 工具) · 用法

**id**　f54bf8fae60582d3e7-3

**english**　Find Point Clusters

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html

**text**　此地理處理工具適用于 ArcGIS Enterprise 10.6.1 或更高版本。
查找點聚類的輸入為點圖層。 此工具從輸入點圖層中提取聚類，并標識所有周圍噪點。
查找點聚類要求將輸入點圖層投影或輸出座標系 設置為投影座標系統。
提供兩個聚類方法參數選項。 定義距離 (DBSCAN) 使用 DBSCAN 算法并基于指定搜索距離查找緊密鄰近的點聚類。 自調整 (HDBSCAN) 使用 HDBSCAN 算法（適用于 ArcGIS Enterprise 10.7 及更高版本）并基于聚類概率（或穩定性）查找點聚類，方法與 DBSCAN 類似，不同之處在于使用適用于具有可變密度的聚類的可變距離。 如果選擇了 DBSCAN，則只能在二維空間中找到聚類，或在空間和時間中都可以找到聚類。 如果選中使用時間查找聚類，同時輸入圖層啟用了時間且類型為時刻，則 DBSCAN 將基于指定的搜索距離和搜索持續時間（適用于 ArcGIS Enterprise 10.8 及更高版本）來查找緊密鄰近的點的時空聚類。
每個聚類的最小圖徵數參數的使用方式不同，具體取決于聚類方法： 定義距離 (DBSCAN)—指定在某點開始形成聚類的搜索距離內必須找到的圖徵數。 結果可能包括圖徵數量少于此值的聚類。 搜索距離可使用搜索距離參數進行設置。 使用時間查找聚類時，需要搜索持續時間。 在搜索聚類成員時，必須在搜索距離和搜索持續時間內找到每個聚類的最小圖徵數以形成聚類。 請注意，此距離和持續時間與所找到的點聚類的直徑或時間范圍無關。 自調整 (HDBSCAN)—指定與估算密度時考慮的每個點相鄰的圖徵數（包括該點）。 此數字也是提取聚類時所允許的最小聚類大小。
此工具可生成包含新整型欄位 CLUSTER_ID 的輸出圖徵類，可識別各個圖徵所在的聚類。 默認渲染基于 COLOR_ID 欄位。 將為每個顏色分配多個聚類。 將分配顏色并重復使用，以使每個聚類的外觀不同于其鄰近聚類。
如果定義距離 (DBSCAN) 聚類方法與時間一同用于查找時空聚類，則結果中還將包含以下欄位： FEAT_TIME —每個圖徵的原始時刻時間。 START_DATETIME—圖徵所屬聚類的時間范圍的開始時間。 END_DATETIME—圖徵所屬聚類的時間范圍的結束時間。 結果圖層的時間屬性將設置為 START_DATETIME 和 END_DATETIME 欄位上的間隔，以確保在使用時間滑塊可視化時空聚類時將所有聚類成員繪制在一起。 這些欄位僅用于可視化。 對于噪點圖徵，START_DATETIME 和 END_DATETIME 將等于 FEAT_TIME。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#usage

## 查找點聚類 (GeoAnalytics Server 工具) · 用法

**id**　f54bf8fae60582d3e7-4

**english**　Find Point Clusters

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html

**text**　如果聚類方法為自調整 (HDBSCAN)，則輸出圖徵類也將包含以下欄位： PROB—圖徵屬于其所分配聚類的概率。 OUTLIER—圖徵在其自己的聚類中為異常值的可能性。 值越大表示該圖徵越有可能是異常值。 EXEMPLAR—最能代表每個聚類的圖徵。 這些圖徵以值 1 表示。 STABILITY—每個聚類在一定比例范圍內的持久性。 值越大表示聚類在越大的距離比例范圍內持續存在。
在使用具有包含超過 300 萬個圖徵的輸入圖層的 HDBSCAN 算法時，除非管理員增加 GeoAnalyticsTools GP 服務 上的 javaHeapSize 參數值，否則該工具可能會失敗。 每 300 萬個圖徵需要約 2 GB 的堆空間。 除 GeoAnalytics Server 通常要求的 16 GB 外，每臺 GeoAnalytics Server 計算機還應具有由 javaHeapSize 指定的 RAM 空間。 例如，要使用 HDBSCAN 聚集 900 萬個圖徵，則將 javaHeapSize 設置為不小于 6144 MB（即 6 GB）。 在這種情況下，每臺 GeoAnalytics Server 計算機應具有總共至少 22 GB 的可用 RAM。
您可以通過以下提示來提高查找點聚類工具的性能： 設置范圍環境，以便僅分析感興趣的資料。 有選擇性地選擇搜索距離和持續時間。 在相同資料上使用較小的搜索距離或半徑可能性能更優。 使用與分析運行位置同屬 本地的資料。
該地理處理工具由 ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server 提供支持。 分析將在 GeoAnalytics Server 上完成，且結果將存儲在 ArcGIS Enterprise 的內容中。
運行 GeoAnalytics Server 工具時，分析將在 GeoAnalytics Server 上完成。 要獲得最佳性能，請通過在 ArcGIS Enterprise 門戶上托管的圖徵圖層或通過大資料文件共享，使 GeoAnalytics Server 可以訪問資料。 非 GeoAnalytics Server 本地的資料，將在分析開始前移動至 GeoAnalytics Server。 這意味著運行工具需要更長時間，并且在某些情況下，從 ArcGIS Pro 到 GeoAnalytics Server 移動資料可能會失敗。 失敗的閾值取決于網絡速度，以及資料的大小和復雜性。 建議您始終共享資料或創建大資料文件共享。 了解有關將資料共享至您的門戶的詳細信息 了解有關通過 Server Manager 創建大資料文件共享的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#usage

**id**　f54bf8fae60582d3e7-11

**english**　Find Point Clusters

**parameter**　clustering_method

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 聚類方法 （可選）
- 指定將用于定義聚類的方法。 定義的距離 (DBSCAN)—使用指定距離將密集聚類與稀疏噪點相分離。 DBSCAN 是最快的聚類方法，但僅適用于距離明確的情況，并且非常適用于定義可能存在的所有聚類。 此方法將產生密度相似的聚類。 這是默認設置。 自調整 (HDBSCAN)—使用可變距離將不同密度的聚類與稀疏噪點相分離。 HDBSCAN 是最以資料為驅動的聚類方法，且需要的用戶輸入最少。
- String

- 名稱

- minimum_points
- 根據選擇的聚類方法，此參數的使用方式也不同，如下所示： 定義距離 (DBSCAN)—指定在某點開始形成聚類的特定距離內必須找到的圖徵數。 距離可使用搜索距離參數進行定義。 自調整 (HDBSCAN)—指定與估算密度時考慮的每個點相鄰的圖徵數（包括該點）。 此數字也是提取聚類時所允許的最小聚類大小。
- Long

- 指定將用于存儲輸出的 ArcGIS Data Store。 在時空大資料存儲中存儲的所有結果都將存儲在 WGS84 中。 在關系資料存儲中存儲的結果都將保持各自的座標系。 SPATIOTEMPORAL_DATA_STORE—輸出將存儲在時空大資料存儲中。 這是默認設置。 RELATIONAL_DATA_STORE—輸出將存儲在關系資料存儲中。
- String

## 查找點聚類 (GeoAnalytics Server 工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　f54bf8fae60582d3e7-21

**english**　Find Point Clusters

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html

**parameter**　clustering_method

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- clustering_method （可選）
- 指定將用于定義聚類的方法。 DBSCAN—使用指定距離將密集聚類與稀疏噪點相分離。 DBSCAN 是最快的聚類方法，但僅適用于距離明確的情況，并且非常適用于定義可能存在的所有聚類。 此方法將產生密度相似的聚類。 這是默認設置。 HDBSCAN—使用可變距離將不同密度的聚類與稀疏噪點相分離。 HDBSCAN 是最以資料為驅動的聚類方法，且需要的用戶輸入最少。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#derived-output

## 查找點聚類 (GeoAnalytics Server 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　f54bf8fae60582d3e7-26

**english**　Find Point Clusters

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html

**text**　#-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Name: FindPointClusters.py
# Description: Finds Point Clusters of rodent infestations
#
# Requirements: ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set local variables
inputPoints = "https://myGeoAnalyticsMachine.domain.com/geoanalytics/rest/services/DataStoreCatalogs/bigDataFileShares_countyData/BigDataCatalogServer/rat_sightings"
minimumPoints = 10
outputName = "RodentClusters"
searchDistance = "1 Kilometers"
dataStore = "SPATIOTEMPORAL_DATA_STORE"
clusterMethod = "DBSCAN"

# Run Find Point Clusters
arcpy.geoanalytics.FindPointClusters(inputPoints, outputName, mimimumPoints,
                                     searchDistance, dataStore, clusterMethod)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html

**id**　f54bf8fae60582d3e7-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Find Point Clusters，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入點圖層 [input_points]、輸出名稱 [output_name]、每個聚類的最小圖徵數 [minimum_points]、搜索距離 [search_distance]、資料存儲 （可選） [data_store]、聚類方法 （可選） [clustering_method]、使用時間查找聚類 （可選） [use_time]、搜索持續時間 （可選） [search_duration]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#parameters

**english**　Find Point Clusters

**id**　f54bf8fae60582d3e7-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/find-point-clusters.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "input_points", "label_zh": "輸入點圖層", "label_en": "Input Point Layer", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Set", "description_en": "The point feature class containing the point clusters."}, {"parameter": "output_name", "label_zh": "輸出名稱", "label_en": "Output Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the output feature service."}, {"parameter": "minimum_points", "label_zh": "每個聚類的最小圖徵數", "label_en": "Minimum Features per Cluster", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "This parameter is used differently depending on the clustering method chosen as follows: Defined distance (DBSCAN)—Specifies the number of features that must be found within a certain distance of a point for that point to start to form a cluster. The distance is defined using the Search Distance parameter. Self-adjusting (HDBSCAN)—Specifies the number of features neighboring each point (including the point) that will be considered when estimating density. This number is also the minimum cluster size allowed when extracting clusters."}, {"parameter": "search_distance", "label_zh": "搜索距離", "label_en": "Search Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The maximum distance that will be considered. The Minimum Features per Cluster value specified must be found within this distance for cluster membership. Individual clusters will be separated by at least this distance. If a feature is located farther than this distance from the next closest feature in the cluster, it will not be included in the cluster."}, {"parameter": "data_store", "label_zh": "資料存儲 （可選）", "label_en": "Data Store (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the ArcGIS Data Store where the output will be stored. All results stored in a spatiotemporal big data store will be stored in WGS84. Results stored in a relational data store will maintain their coordinate system. Spatiotemporal big data store—Output will be stored in a spatiotemporal big data store. This is the default. Relational data store—Output will be stored in a relational data store."}, {"parameter": "clustering_method", "label_zh": "聚類方法 （可選）", "label_en": "Clustering Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the method that will be used to define clusters. Defined distance (DBSCAN)—Uses a specified distance to separate dense clusters from sparser noise. DBSCAN is the fastest of the clustering methods but is only appropriate if there is a clear distance that works well to define all clusters that may be present. This results in clusters that have similar densities. This is the default. Self-adjusting (HDBSCAN)—Uses varying distances to separate clusters of varying densities from sparser noise. HDBSCAN is the most data driven of the clustering methods and requires the least user input."}, {"parameter": "use_time", "label_zh": "使用時間查找聚類 （可選）", "label_en": "Use Time to Find Clusters (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether or not time will be used to discover clusters with DBSCAN. Checked—Spatiotemporal clusters will be found using both a search distance and a search duration. Unchecked—Spatial clusters will be found using a search distance and time will be ignored. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "search_duration", "label_zh": "搜索持續時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Search Duration (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Time Unit", "description_en": "When searching for cluster members, the specified minimum number of points must be found within this time duration to form a cluster."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.geoanalytics.FindPointClusters(input_points, output_name, minimum_points, {search_distance}, {data_store}, {clustering_method}, {use_time}, {search_duration})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：input_points、output_name、minimum_points、search_distance、data_store、clustering_method、use_time、search_duration。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 將點匯總為線段(分析工具) · 使用率

**id**　f54ead7eb7f25ecf69-2

**english**　Summarize Points To Segments

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 分析工具箱
- 統計工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html

**text**　輸出將包括一個層, 幫助您直觀分析結果。 默認情況下, 這將使用 COUNT 統計來顯示有分級顏色的線條段。 注意無效值屬于“ 超出范圍” 類別。 當片段長度參數值大于給定輸入行段時, 輸入行段不會被分割。 使用 Unsplit 線條參數來綜合具有同步端點的線條特征。 在檢查未拆線參數時, 輸出特性類將包含以下額外欄位: FID\ INPUT DATASSET NAME > 是含有原始輸入資料集對象的文本欄位。 FID\ INPUTASTET NAPE ⁇ Unsplit是一個整數欄位,它包含在未分割行操作完成后的新對象ID。指定輸出線條參數值將創建含有 FID_Unsplit 和 FID_Orig 欄位的表格。要保存輸入線資料集中的屬性資料(例如街道名稱),請做以下工作: 在 FID_Orig 欄位上,從輸出線條表到輸入線條資料組的 Orig 欄位上設置屬性連接工具(例如使用添加附加符)。在輸出線條資料組的 Olig 欄位中,讓第二個屬性加入輸出特征類的FID {INPUTAST DATASSETNAMET_NE}{Unsplit域,與輸出線條表的 FID_Unsplit域連接。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The output will include a layer to help you visualize the result of the analysis. By default, this will show line segments with graduated colors using the COUNT statistic. Note that null values fall under the \"out of range\" category.\nWhen the Segment Length parameter value is greater than a given input line segment, the input line segment will not be split.\nUse the Unsplit Lines parameter to aggregate line features that have coincident endpoints.\nWhen the Unsplit Lines parameter is checked, the output feature class will contain the following additional fields: FID_<INPUT DATASET NAME> is a text field that contains the ObjectIDs of the original input dataset. FID_<INPUT DATASET NAME>_Unsplit is an integer field that contains the new ObjectIDs after the Unsplit Lines operation is performed.\nSpecifying an Output Lineage Table parameter value will create a table that contains the FID_Unsplit and FID_Orig fields. To preserve attribute data (for example, street names) from the input line datasets, do the following: Make an attribute join (for example, using the Add Join) tool on the FID_Orig field from the output lineage table to the ObjectID field of the input lines dataset. Make a second attribute join on the FID_<INPUT DATASET NAME>_Unsplit field of the output feature class with the FID_Unsplit field of the output lineage table.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#usage

## 將點匯總為線段(分析工具) · 使用率

**id**　f54ead7eb7f25ecf69-3

**english**　Summarize Points To Segments

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 分析工具箱
- 統計工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html

**text**　使用實地地圖參數控制輸入摘要點欄位的屬性信息如何被傳輸到目標資料集。默認情況下,將創建一個COUNT摘要點的COUNT欄位。使用一個動作來決定從輸入資料集中一個或多個欄位的數值如何映射到目標資料集中的單個欄位。可用的動作是第一、最后、Contate、Sum、Memo、Memon、Mod、最低、最大、標準偏離和計算。當超過一個特性匹配到一個摘要交叉點時,外地地圖參數中指定的動作僅適用于對概要點的屬性。例如,如果三個特性與36、47和22的DRIVER_AG屬性值進行匯總,以及“Mode”將應用輸出欄位的值為 35 。 在統計計算時忽略了摘要點欄位中的數值。 例如, 15.5 無效值和 2.5 將導致9.0 平均值和 2.2 計數。使用 Concatenatet 動作時, 您可以指定一個分隔符, 如逗號或其他字符。 單擊“ 分隔符” 文本框的起始部分添加分隔符字符。 標準偏離動作對單個輸入值不有效。 使用導出選項保存欄位地圖為.fieldmap 文件。 使用裝入選項來裝載.fielemap 文件。 文件中指定的特性層或資料集必須與工具中所使用的資料集匹配。 否則, 欄位映射參數 。將重新設置。在文本源欄位上使用“剪切文本”按鈕,選擇從輸入值中提取哪些字符到目標欄位。要訪問“剪切文本”按鈕,請在輸入欄位列表的文字欄位上徘徊;然后指定起始和結尾字符位置。欄位也可以在 Python 腳本中映射。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Use the Field Map parameter to control how the attribute information from the input summary points fields are transferred to the target dataset. By default, a COUNT field of summary points will be created.\nUse an action to determine how the values from one or multiple fields from the input dataset will be mapped to a single field from the target dataset.\nThe available actions are First, Last, Concatenate, Sum, Mean, Median, Mode, Minimum, Maximum, Standard Deviation, and Count. Actions specified in the Field Map parameter only apply to attributes the summary points when more than one feature is matched to a summary intersection. For example, if three features with DRIVER_AGE attribute values of 36, 47, and 22 are summarized for a given intersection, and the Mean action is applied, the output field will have a value of 35. Null values in summary point fields are ignored for statistic calculation. For example, 15.5, a null value, and 2.5 will result in mean of 9.0 and a count of 2. When using the Concatenate action, you can specify a delimiter such as a comma or other characters. Click the start of the Delimiter text box to add the delimiter characters. The Standard Deviation action is not valid for single input values. Use the Export option to save a field map as a .fieldmap file. Use the Load option to load a .fieldmap file. The feature layer or dataset specified in the file must match the dataset used in the tool. Otherwise, the Field Map parameter will be reset. Use the Slice Text button on text source fields to choose which characters from an input value will be extracted to the target field. To access the Slice Text button, hover over a text field in the input fields list; then specify the start and end character positions. Fields can also be mapped in a Python script.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#usage

## 將點匯總為線段(分析工具) · 參數

**id**　f54ead7eb7f25ecf69-9

**english**　Summarize Points To Segments

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 分析工具箱
- 統計工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#parameters

**parameter**　distance_method

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 遠程方法(可選)
- 指定是否將使用人造衛星(地球)距離法或平地(行星)距離法上最短的路徑。建議您使用大地測量方法,用于儲存在不適合遠程測量的協調系統(例如網絡Mercator或任何地理座標系統)的資料和跨大地理區域的資料集中的資料。各功能之間將使用平面-行星距離。各特征之間將使用大地測量-地質距離。這種方法考慮到鼠標曲線的曲直,并正確處理國際日期線和極附近資料。這是默認的。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Distance Method (Optional)", "Specifies whether a shortest path on a spheroid (geodesic) or a flat earth (planar) distance method will be used. - 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- search_radius (可選擇)
- 用于搜索近方特征的半徑。 如果沒有指定值, 所有摘要點都將被評估為接近每個段段。 如果指定了距離但沒有單位或未知, 將使用輸入特性協調系統的各個單位。 如果GEODESIC 選項被用于遠程方法參數, 請使用公里或英里等直線單位。
- 線線股

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["search_radius (Optional)", "The radius that will be used to search for near features. If no value is specified, all summary points will be evaluated for proximity to each segment. If a distance but no unit or unknown is specified, the units of the coordinate system of the input features will be used. If the GEODESIC option is used for the distance_method parameter, use a linear unit such as kilometers or miles.", "Linear Unit"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#parameters

**english**　Summarize Points To Segments

- 統計工具

- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 距離_ 方法( 可選)
- 指定是否將使用人造衛星(地球)距離法或平地(平板)距離法上最短的路徑。建議您使用GEODESIC方法儲存在不適合遠程測量的協調系統(例如網絡Mercator或任何地理座標系統)的資料和跨大地理區域的資料集中的資料。PLAAR-Planar距離將在各功能之間使用。GEODESIC-Geodecic 距離將在各功能間使用。這種方法考慮到鼠標的曲縮,并正確處理國際日期線和極附近資料。這是默認的。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["distance_method (Optional)", "Specifies whether a shortest path on a spheroid (geodesic) or a flat earth (planar) distance method will be used. It is recommended that you use the GEODESIC method for data stored in a coordinate system that is not appropriate for distance measurements (for example, Web Mercator or any geographic coordinate system) and for a dataset that spans a large geographic area. PLANAR—Planar distance will be used between features. GEODESIC—Geodesic distance will be used between features. This method takes into account the curvature of the spheroid and correctly deals with data near the international date line and the poles. This is the default.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**source_language**　en

**text**　import arcpy
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:/study_area.gdb"

arcpy.analysis.SummarizePointsToSegments(
    in_lines="major_roads",
    in_sum_points="vehicle_crashes",
    out_feature_class=r"summarized_segments",
    search_radius="50 Feet",
    distance_method="GEODESIC",
    segment_length="250 Feet",
    out_table=None,
    field_mapping='COUNT "COUNT" true true false 0 Long 0 0,Count,#,crash_merge_clip_SMALL,OBJECTID,-1,-1',
    unsplit_lines="NOT_UNSPLIT_LINES",
    out_lineage_table=None
)

## 將點匯總為線段(分析工具) · 代碼樣本

**id**　f54ead7eb7f25ecf69-29

**english**　Summarize Points To Segments

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 分析工具箱
- 統計工具

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html

**text**　# Name: SummarizePointsToSegments.py
# Description: Summarize vehicle crashes for 250-foot segments of roadway 
#              centerlines

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set workspace environment
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data/collision_data.gdb"

# Set required parameters
in_lines = "roadway_centerlines"
in_sum_points = "collisions_2010_2020"
out_feature_class = "summarized_intersections"

# Optional parameters
search_radius = "50 Feet"
distance_method = "GEODESIC"
segment_length = "250 Feet"
out_table = ""
field_mapping = r'COUNT "COUNT" true true false 0 Long 0 0,Count,#,crash_merge_clip_SMALL OBJECTID,-1,-1;'
unsplit_lines="UNSPLIT_LINES"
out_lineage_table=""

arcpy.analysis.SummarizePointsToSegments(
    in_lines, in_sum_points, out_feature_class, search_radius, distance_method,
    segment_length, out_table, field_mapping, unsplit_lines, out_lineage_table)

## 將點匯總為線段(分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 分析工具箱
- 統計工具

**english**　Summarize Points To Segments

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html

**id**　f54ead7eb7f25ecf69-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Summarize Points To Segments，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入線 [in_lines]、用于匯總的輸入點 [in_sum_points]、輸出功能類 [out_feature_class]、搜索半徑( 可選) [search_radius]、遠程方法(可選) [distance_method]、線段長度( 可選擇) [segment_length]、產出表(備選) [out_table]、(備選) [field_mapping]、未拆開的線條( 可選) [unsplit_lines]、產出直線表(備選) [out_lineage_table]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#parameters

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#parameters

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/analysis/summarize-points-to-segments.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_lines", "label_zh": "輸入線", "label_en": "Input Lines", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input lines that will be summarized."}, {"parameter": "in_sum_points", "label_zh": "用于匯總的輸入點", "label_en": "Input Points to Summarize", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input points to be summarized."}, {"parameter": "out_feature_class", "label_zh": "輸出功能類", "label_en": "Output Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output feature class that contains the summary attributes from the Input Points to Summarize parameter value."}, {"parameter": "search_radius", "label_zh": "搜索半徑( 可選)", "label_en": "Search Radius (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The radius that will be used to search for near features. If no value is specified, all summary points will be evaluated for proximity to each segment. If a distance but no unit or unknown is specified, the units of the coordinate system of the input features will be used. If the Geodesic option is used for the Distance Method parameter, use a linear unit such as kilometers or miles."}, {"parameter": "distance_method", "label_zh": "遠程方法(可選)", "label_en": "Distance Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether a shortest path on a spheroid (geodesic) or a flat earth (planar) distance method will be used. It is recommended that you use the Geodesic method for data stored in a coordinate system that is not appropriate for distance measurements (for example, Web Mercator or any geographic coordinate system) and for a dataset that spans a large geographic area. Planar—Planar distance will be used between features. Geodesic—Geodesic distance will be used between features. This method takes into account the curvature of the spheroid and correctly deals with data near the international date line and the poles. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "segment_length", "label_zh": "線段長度( 可選擇)", "label_en": "Segment Length (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The length of line segments in the output."}, {"parameter": "out_table", "label_zh": "產出表(備選)", "label_en": "Output Table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The output table containing proximity information from the input features and summary points."}, {"parameter": "field_mapping", "label_zh": "(備選)", "label_en": "Summary Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field Mappings", "description_en": "Use the field map control to add, delete, rename, and reorder fields, as well as change other field properties. By default, the field map will add a COUNT field to the output feature class. This will show the counts of input points for each given intersection point or line segment. The field map can also be used to combine values from two or more input fields."}, {"parameter": "unsplit_lines", "label_zh": "未拆開的線條( 可選)", "label_en": "Unsplit Lines (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether line features that have coincident endpoints will be aggregated. Checked—Input lines will be aggregated with coincident endpoints. Unchecked—Input lines with not be aggregated with coincident endpoints."}, {"parameter": "out_lineage_table", "label_zh": "產出直線表(備選)", "label_en": "Output Lineage Table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "A lineage table that records the input features that participate in the creation of each single part output feature."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.analysis.SummarizePointsToSegments(in_lines, in_sum_points, out_feature_class, {search_radius}, {distance_method}, {segment_length}, {out_table}, {field_mapping}, {unsplit_lines}, {out_lineage_table})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_lines、in_sum_points、out_feature_class、search_radius、distance_method、segment_length、out_table、field_mapping、unsplit_lines、out_lineage_table。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**text**　此工具會修改輸入資料。 有關詳細信息以及避免意外更改資料的策略，請參閱 修改或更新輸入資料的工具。
對于每組相同的記錄，該工具會刪除除第一個相同記錄之外的所有相同記錄。 相同記錄的順序將與查找相同項工具返回的記錄的順序相同。
將對輸入資料集中多個欄位的值進行比較。 如果指定了多個欄位，則記錄將與第一個欄位中的值匹配，然后與第二個欄位的值匹配，依此類推。
對于圖徵類或圖徵圖層輸入，請使用欄位參數中的幾何欄位來比較圖徵幾何，以按位置查找相同的圖徵。 僅當幾何欄位被選作其中一個輸入欄位時，XY 容差和 Z 容差參數才有效。
可選輸出重復 ID 映射表值將包含以下欄位： IN_FID—輸入中具有重復項的記錄的對象 ID REPR_FID—保留的代表性記錄的對象 ID

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/delete-identical.html#usage

## 刪除相同項 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　f5625df7d902fa9138-20

**english**　Delete Identical

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 常規工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/delete-identical.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/delete-identical.html

**text**　# Name: DeleteIdentical_Example2.py
# Description: Delete identical features in a dataset based on Shape (geometry) and a TEXT field.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set workspace environment
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data/sbfire.gdb"

# Set input feature class
in_dataset = "fireincidents"

# Set the field on which the identical records are found
fields = ["Shape", "INTENSITY"]

# Set the XY tolerance within which identical records will be deleted
xy_tol = "0.02 Miles"

# Set the Z tolerance to default
z_tol = ""

# Run Delete Identical
arcpy.management.DeleteIdentical(in_dataset, fields, xy_tol, z_tol)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/delete-identical.html#derived-output

## 刪除相同項 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 常規工具集

**english**　Delete Identical

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/delete-identical.html

**id**　f5625df7d902fa9138-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/delete-identical.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Delete Identical，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入資料集 [in_dataset]、欄位 [fields]、XY 容差 （可選） [xy_tolerance]、Z 容差 （可選） [z_tolerance]、輸出重復 ID 映射表 （可選） [out_mapping_table]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/delete-identical.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_dataset", "label_zh": "輸入資料集", "label_en": "Input Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table View", "description_en": "The table or feature class that will have identical records deleted."}, {"parameter": "fields", "label_zh": "欄位", "label_en": "Fields", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field or fields whose values will be compared to find identical records."}, {"parameter": "xy_tolerance", "label_zh": "XY 容差 （可選）", "label_en": "XY Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The x,y tolerance that will be applied to each vertex when evaluating whether there is an identical vertex in another feature."}, {"parameter": "z_tolerance", "label_zh": "Z 容差 （可選）", "label_en": "Z Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The z-tolerance that will be applied to each vertex when evaluating whether there is an identical vertex in another feature."}, {"parameter": "out_mapping_table", "label_zh": "輸出重復 ID 映射表 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Duplicate IDs Mapping Table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table View", "description_en": "An optional output table that will include the object ID values of all records from the input that have a duplicate, matched with the object ID values of the representative record that was retained."}]

**signatures**

## 批量計算統計資料 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　f572162f589057f1da-2

**english**　Batch Calculate Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 網格屬性工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/batch-calculate-statistics.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/batch-calculate-statistics.html

**text**　在計算統計值時使用的那部分網格由跳躍因子控制。 特定輸入值可指示水平或垂直跳躍因子，值為 1 時使用每個像素，值為 2 時則每隔一個像素使用一個。 此跳躍因子的取值范圍只能從 1 至網格中列/行的數量。
為 Esri Grid 和 RADARSAT2 格式計算統計資料時始終使用跳躍因子 1。
存儲在文件地理資料庫或企業級地理資料庫中的網格資料集的跳躍因子非常不同。 首先，如果 x 和 y 跳躍因子不同，則使用兩者中較小的一個來作為 x 和 y 共同的跳躍因子。 其次，跳躍因子與最貼合所選擇跳躍因子的金字塔等級相關。 如果跳躍因子值不等于金字塔中的像素數（例如，跳躍因子為5，而最近的金字塔等級為 4×4 像素，即等級 2），軟件會下舍入到下一個金字塔等級（在此情況下為 2），并將此值用作跳躍因子。
跳躍因子不適用于所有網格格式。 可計算統計資料并利用跳躍因子的網格格式包括 TIFF、IMG、NITF、DTED、RAW、ADRG、CIB、CADRG、DIGEST、GIS、LAN、CIT、COT、ERMapper、ENVI DAT、BIL、BIP、BSQ 和地理資料庫。
“忽略值”選項可用于排除統計資料計算中的特定值。 您可能想要忽略 NoData 值，或讓您的計算出現偏度的值。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/batch-calculate-statistics.html#usage

- 標注

**values**　
- X 跳躍因子 （可選）
- 樣本之間水平像素的數量。 在計算統計值時使用的那部分網格由跳躍因子控制。 特定輸入值可指示水平或垂直跳躍因子，值為 1 時使用每個像素，值為 2 時則每隔一個像素使用一個。 此跳躍因子的取值范圍只能從 1 至網格中列/行的數量。 此值必須大于零并小于等于網格中的列數。 默認值為 1 或者為上次使用的跳躍因子。 存儲在文件地理資料庫或企業級地理資料庫中的網格資料集的跳躍因子不同。 首先，如果 x 和 y 跳躍因子不同，則使用兩者中較小的一個來作為 x 和 y 共同的跳躍因子。 其次，跳躍因子與最貼合所選擇跳躍因子的金字塔等級相關。 如果跳躍因子不等于金字塔圖層中像素的數量，則該數量向下舍入至下一個金字塔等級，并使用那些統計值。
- Long

**parameter**　Number_of_columns_to_skip

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Number_of_columns_to_skip （可選）
- 樣本之間水平像素的數量。 在計算統計值時使用的那部分網格由跳躍因子控制。 特定輸入值可指示水平或垂直跳躍因子，值為 1 時使用每個像素，值為 2 時則每隔一個像素使用一個。 此跳躍因子的取值范圍只能從 1 至網格中列/行的數量。 此值必須大于零并小于等于網格中的列數。 默認值為 1 或者為上次使用的跳躍因子。 存儲在文件地理資料庫或企業級地理資料庫中的網格資料集的跳躍因子不同。 首先，如果 x 和 y 跳躍因子不同，則使用兩者中較小的一個來作為 x 和 y 共同的跳躍因子。 其次，跳躍因子與最貼合所選擇跳躍因子的金字塔等級相關。 如果跳躍因子不等于金字塔圖層中像素的數量，則該數量向下舍入至下一個金字塔等級，并使用那些統計值。
- Long

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/batch-calculate-statistics.html#derived-output

## 批量計算統計資料 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　f572162f589057f1da-20

**english**　Batch Calculate Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 網格屬性工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/batch-calculate-statistics.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/batch-calculate-statistics.html

**text**　# Calculate Statistics for multiple raster datasets with
# multiple ignore values.
# Skip datasets that already have the statistics.

import arcpy
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/Workspace"

inras = ["image1.tif", "image2.img", "fgdb.gdb/image3"]
skipcol = 5
skiprow = 5
ignoreval = [0, 255, 21]
skipexist = "SKIP_EXISTING"

arcpy.management.BatchCalculateStatistics(
     inras, skipcol, skiprow, ignoreval,skipexist)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/batch-calculate-statistics.html#derived-output
